how is sonata delivered?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:

Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.route'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'sonata']]}

how much does other gastrostomy cost?
----------
PREDS:

----------
TRUTH:

what is gentamicin's price?
----------
PREDS:
select distinct cost.cost from cost where cost.event_type = 'prescriptions' and cost.event_id in ( select prescriptions.row_id from prescriptions where prescriptions.drug = 'gentamicin' )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['cost.cost', 'prescriptions.row_id'], 'tab': ['cost', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['cost.event_type', '=', 'prescriptions'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'gentamicin']]}
----------
TRUTH:

tell me the percentile of the hematocrit, calculated value of 37.0 among patients of the same age as patient 97395 during their current hospital encounter?
----------
PREDS:
select distinct t1.c1 from ( select labevents.valuenum, percent_rank() over ( order by labevents.valuenum ) as c1 from labevents where labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'hematocrit' ) and labevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.age = ( select admissions.age from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 97395 and admissions.dischtime is null ) ) ) as t1 where t1.valuenum = 37.0
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.age', 'admissions.hadm_id', 'd_labitems.itemid', 'labevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '97395'], ['d_labitems.label', '=', 'hematocrit'], ['t1.valuenum', '=', '37.0']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select distinct t1.c1 from ( select labevents.valuenum, percent_rank() over ( order by labevents.valuenum ) as c1 from labevents where labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'hematocrit, calculated' ) and labevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.age = ( select admissions.age from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 97395 and admissions.dischtime is null ) ) ) as t1 where t1.valuenum = 37.0

show me the two year survival probability of the patients diagnosed with headache?
----------
PREDS:
select sum( case when patients.dod is null then 1 when strftime('%j',patients.dod) - strftime('%j',t2.charttime) > 2 * 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'headache' ) group by admissions.subject_id having min(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%j',current_time) - strftime('%j',t1.charttime) > 2 * 365 ) as t2 join patients on t2.subject_id = patients.subject_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'sum', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'headache'], ["strftime('%j','2105-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',t1.charttime)", '>', '2 * 365']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select sum( case when patients.dod is null then 1 when strftime('%j',patients.dod) - strftime('%j',t2.charttime) > 2 * 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'headache' ) group by admissions.subject_id having min(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%j',current_time) - strftime('%j',t1.charttime) > 2 * 365 ) as t2 join patients on t2.subject_id = patients.subject_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'sum', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.short_title', '=', 'headache'], ["strftime('%j','2105-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',t1.charttime)", '>', '2 * 365']]}
----------

give me the four year survival probability of patients who were prescribed dexamethasone after having been diagnosed with alcohol cirrhosis liver?
----------
PREDS:
select sum( case when patients.dod is null then 1 when strftime('%j',patients.dod) - strftime('%j',t4.charttime) > 4 * 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) from ( select t2.subject_id, t2.charttime from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'alcoholic cirrhosis of liver' ) group by admissions.subject_id having min(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%j',current_time) - strftime('%j',t1.charttime) > 4 * 365 ) as t2 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'dexamethasone' ) as t3 on t2.subject_id = t3.subject_id where t2.charttime < t3.startdate ) as t4 join patients on t4.subject_id = patients.subject_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'sum', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'prescriptions.startdate', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.charttime', 't2.subject_id', 't4.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'alcoholic cirrhosis of liver'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'dexamethasone'], ["strftime('%j','2105-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',t1.charttime)", '>', '4 * 365'], ['t2.charttime', '<', 't3.startdate']]}
----------
TRUTH:

how many patients died after having been diagnosed with intestinal obstruct nos in the same hospital visit this year?
----------
PREDS:
select count( distinct t2.subject_id ) from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime, t1.hadm_id from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'intestinal obstruct nos' ) ) as t1 group by t1.subject_id having min(t1.charttime) = t1.charttime and strftime('%Y',t1.charttime) = strftime('%Y',current_date) ) as t2 join ( select patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod from admissions join patients on patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id where patients.dod is not null and strftime('%Y',patients.dod) = strftime('%Y',current_date) ) as t3 on t2.subject_id = t3.subject_id where t2.hadm_id = t3.hadm_id

TRUTH:

----------

how many patients expired within 2 months after being diagnosed with late effect cv dis nos since 4 years ago?
----------
PREDS:
select count( distinct t2.subject_id ) from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'late effect cv dis nos' ) ) as t1 group by t1.subject_id having min(t1.charttime) = t1.charttime and datetime(t1.charttime) <= datetime(current_time,'-4 year') ) as t2 join ( select patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod from admissions join patients on patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id where patients.dod is not null and datetime(patients.dod) <= datetime(current_time,'-4 year') ) as t3 on t2.subject_id = t3.subject_id where ( datetime(t3.dod) between datetime(t2.charttime) and datetime(t2.charttime,'+2 month') ) or ( datetime(t2.charttime) between datetime(t3.dod) and datetime(t3.dod,'+2 month') )

----------
PREDS:

----------
TRUTH:

retrieve the top five most common diagnoses?
----------
PREDS:
select t1.icd9_code from ( select diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from diagnoses_icd group by diagnoses_icd.icd9_code ) as t1 where t1.c1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['diagnoses_icd.icd9_code', 't1.icd9_code'], 'tab': ['diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['t1.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code in ( select t1.icd9_code from ( select diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from diagnoses_icd group by diagnoses_icd.icd9_code ) as t1 where t1.c1 <= 5 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.short_title', 'diagnoses_icd.icd9_code', 't1.icd9_code'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['t1.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------

among the patients with age 20s since 3 years ago, what were the top five most frequent diagnoses.
----------

what were the five diagnoses that had the lowest five year survival rate.
PRED: select d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code in ( select t4.icd9_code from ( select t3.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by t3.c1 asc ) as c2 from ( select t2.icd9_code, 100 - sum( case when patients.dod is null then 1 when strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',t2.charttime) > 5 * 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) as c1 from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.icd9_code, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id group by admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code having min(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%J','2105-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%J',t1.charttime) > 5 * 365 ) as t2 join patients on t2.subject_id = patients.subject_id group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 ) as t4 where t4.c2 <= 5 )
GOLD: select d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code in ( select t4.icd9_code from ( select t3.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by t3.c1 desc ) as c2 from ( select t2.icd9_code, 100 - sum( case when patients.dod is null then 1 when strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',t2.charttime) > 5 * 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) as c1 from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.icd9_code, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id group by admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code having min(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%J','2105-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%J',t1.charttime) > 5 * 365 ) as t2 join patients on t2.subject_id = patients.subject_id group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 ) as t4 where t4.c2 <= 5 )

GOLD: select d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code in ( select t1.icd9_code from ( select diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from diagnoses_icd where diagnoses_icd.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.age between 20 and 29 ) and datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime('2105-12-31 23:59:00','-3 year') group by diagnoses_icd.icd9_code ) as t1 where t1.c1 <= 5 )
